# Graph Report - betting-model  (2026-11-27)

## Corpus Check
- Large corpus: 2924 files · ~5,221,664 words. Semantic extraction will be expensive (many Claude tokens). Consider running on a subfolder.

## Summary
- 60069 nodes · 149491 edges · 1365 communities (1125 shown, 240 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4319 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 7,979,895 input · 0 output

## Surprising Connections (you probably didn't know these)
- `Market-anchored NCAAF game-state model (Normal remaining margin and total)` --semantically_similar_to--> `StateModel`  [INFERRED] [semantically similar]
  docs/ncaaf_market_audit_inplay.md → models/ncaab_live_ig.py
- `Scratch after the bet is NO_ACTION, row kept` --semantically_similar_to--> `_settle_ufc_picks()`  [INFERRED] [semantically similar]
  docs/horse_racing_scope.md → tracking/paper_tracker.py
- `Stop writing repeats: write a row only when a line appears, moves or disappears` --semantically_similar_to--> `thin_repeats()`  [INFERRED] [semantically similar]
  docs/data_archive.md → data/prune_odds.py
- `Zone shooting-luck gap (gap_total, gap_margin, home and away part)` --semantically_similar_to--> `home_part()`  [INFERRED] [semantically similar]
  docs/nba_search_shotluck.md → models/nba_team_total_luck.py
- `No two live NBA bets on one game that cannot both win` --semantically_similar_to--> `cannot_both_win()`  [INFERRED] [semantically similar]
  docs/sports/nba.md → models/ncaab_live_ig.py
